- **agent-evaluation**: Rigorous testing and benchmarking of LLM agents—covering behavioral contract verification, capability boundary probing, reliability metric collection, and production monitoring. The empirical reality is sobering: even top-ranked agents achieve below 50% on real-world task benchmarks such as SWE-bench Verified and GAIA (Jimenez et al., 2024; Mialon et al., 2023). Evaluation must be deterministic, version-pinned, and contamination-aware to produce claims that compound across releases.
